### [1] [Selective Cotton Boll Localization for Robotic Harvesting: Evaluation of Deep Learning Vision Models Under Field Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19592)
*Thevathayarajh Thayananthan,Xin Zhang,Isuru Laddusinghe Badu,Jonathan Harjono,Glen C. Rains,Beiwen Li,Leonardo M. Bastos,Nuwan K. Wijewardane,Vitor S. Martins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究面向选择性机器人棉花采摘，构建并评估了基于深度学习的感知框架，比较多种检测与分割模型，并通过田间机器人实验验证 YOLOv12-m-seg 的实时检测、分割与选择性采摘能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性机器人棉花采摘需要在复杂自然光照和天气条件下准确检测并分割棉铃，以支持实时、选择性的采摘作业；因此需要评估不同深度学习模型在精度、速度和部署可行性之间的权衡。

Method: 使用三台相机采集并标注 1,008 张田间图像。检测评估 YOLOv8 至 YOLOv13 系列默认配置；分割评估 YOLOv8-seg、YOLOv11-seg、YOLOv12-seg、SAM、SAMv2.1、FastSAM 和 Grounded-SAM+RAM。进一步测试以检测框作为提示的分割方法，并基于面积与人工标注掩码比较。最后使用 UR5e 机械臂、定制末端执行器和 ZED2i 立体相机开展田间采摘实验。

Result: 检测中 GELAN-s 综合 mAP 与速度最佳，mAP 86.1%、precision 81.6%、recall 76.6%、F1 79.0%，平均推理 42.3 ms/图。直接分割中 YOLOv12-m-seg 在 AP@0.5 与 FPS 间最均衡，分割 AP@0.5 为 83.7%，推理 20.4 ms/图。检测提示分割中，GELAN-s 边界框提示改善了 SAM 和 SAMv2.1 的棉铃定位，SAMv2.1 Tiny 持续优于 FastSAM 和 Grounded-SAM+RAM。面积评估中 YOLOv12-m-seg 的 R²=0.966，GELAN-s+SAMv2.1 Tiny 为 0.860。田间实验中 YOLOv12-m-seg 可在不同置信度下完成实时棉铃检测、分割和选择性采摘。

Conclusion: YOLOv12-m-seg 为机器人棉花收获提供了高效感知模型，在精度、速度与田间部署潜力之间表现良好，具备实际应用前景。

Abstract: This study developed and evaluated a deep-learning-based perception framework for selective robotic cotton picking. The dataset contained 1,008 annotated field images collected using three cameras under varying natural lighting and weather conditions. Object-detection models from the YOLOv8 through YOLOv13 families were evaluated using their default configurations, while segmentation performance was assessed using YOLOv8-seg, YOLOv11-seg, YOLOv12-seg, the Segment Anything Model (SAM), SAMv2.1, FastSAM, and Grounded-SAM with the Recognize Anything Model (RAM). Among the detection models, GELAN-s achieved the most favorable balance between mean average precision (mAP) and inference speed, obtaining an mAP of 86.1%, precision of 81.6%, recall of 76.6%, and an F1-score of 79.0%, with an average inference time of 42.3 ms per image. Among the direct segmentation models, YOLOv12-m-seg provided the most favorable balance between AP@0.5 and FPS, achieving a segmentation AP@0.5 of 83.7% with an inference time of 20.4 ms per image. In the detection-prompted segmentation approach, bounding-box prompts generated by GELAN-s improved the localization of cotton bolls for SAM and SAMv2.1, while SAMv2.1 Tiny consistently outperformed FastSAM and Grounded-SAM with RAM. In the area-based evaluation against manually annotated segmentation masks, YOLOv12-m-seg achieved an $R^2$ value of 0.966, compared with 0.860 for GELAN-s + SAMv2.1 Tiny. Field experiments conducted using a UR5e robotic manipulator, a custom end-effector, and a ZED2i stereo camera further validated the effectiveness of the YOLOv12-m-seg model for real-time cotton boll detection, segmentation, and selective picking under varying confidence levels. These results demonstrate that YOLOv12-m-seg provides an efficient perception model for robotic cotton harvesting and has strong potential for field deployment.

</details>


### [2] [PerSeM: Persistent Semantic Memory for Long-Horizon Open-Vocabulary UAV Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19542)
*Saurbh Singh Jamwal,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerSeM 是一个免训练的持久语义记忆框架，用于无人机长时程开放词汇语义建图。它将逐帧语义观测关联到世界空间持久体素，并通过保守精化提升语义正确性与时序稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇分割可为无人机提供丰富语义感知，但逐帧预测在重复观测和视角变化下容易产生时序不一致，不利于长时程语义建图。

Method: PerSeM 将逐帧语义观测关联到持久的世界空间体素，构建基于多数投票的语义记忆，并通过保留历史的空间精化、信任感知回放和上下文引导验证对记忆进行保守精化。

Result: 在 Forest 和 UAVScenes 基准上，持久 3D 记忆相比逐帧预测显著提升语义正确性和时序稳定性；在全部五个 UAVScenes 序列上，PerSeM 在强持久记忆基线之上仍给出一致的额外提升。使用独立于最终预测识别的区域分析表明，增益主要集中在语义困难且时序不稳定的区域，即多数投票记忆最可能仍不确定的区域。

Conclusion: 持久 3D 聚合为长时程语义建图提供了强基础，而对不确定记忆状态进行保守精化可在无需重训练或额外神经网络推理的情况下带来进一步改进。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation enables rich semantic perception for UAVs, but frame-wise predictions can remain temporally inconsistent across repeated observations and changing viewpoints. We present PerSeM, a training-free persistent semantic memory framework for long-horizon open-vocabulary UAV mapping. PerSeM associates frame-wise semantic observations with persistent world-space voxels and constructs a majority-based semantic memory, which is conservatively refined through history-preserving spatial refinement, trust-aware replay, and context-guided verification. Experiments on the Forest and UAVScenes benchmarks show that persistent 3D memory provides substantial gains in semantic correctness and temporal stability over frame-wise predictions. Beyond this strong persistent-memory baseline, PerSeM provides consistent additional improvements, improving both semantic accuracy and temporal stability across all five evaluated UAVScenes sequences. Analysis using regions identified independently of the final PerSeM predictions further shows that these gains are concentrated in semantically difficult and temporally unstable regions, where majority-based memory is most likely to remain uncertain. These results demonstrate that persistent 3D aggregation provides a strong foundation for long-horizon semantic mapping, while conservative refinement of uncertain memory states can provide additional improvements without retraining or additional neural-network inference.

</details>


### [3] [AMB3R-SLAM: Kilometer-scale SLAM with Hierarchical Backend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19518)
*Hengyi Wang,Lourdes Agapito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AMB3R-SLAM，一种可在单张消费级 GPU 上实时运行的稀疏/单目 SLAM 系统，能在超过 1 万帧上重建公里级轨迹，并通过分层后端优化实现全局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SLAM 方法在大规模长序列、动态场景和实时性方面存在不足，尤其是依赖静态世界假设的 bundle adjustment 难以直接处理复杂动态环境，且多传感器扩展能力有限。

Method: 系统采用轻量级前端进行低延迟在线跟踪，并耦合分层后端，逐步施加局部、中层和全局一致性约束；通过避免依赖静态世界假设的 bundle adjustment，自然处理动态场景，并可扩展至 stereo、RGB-D 和 LiDAR 输入。

Result: 在 9 个数据集上取得强相机跟踪性能；相比先前 SOTA，在 VBR 和 Oxford Spires 上绝对轨迹误差（ATE）降低超过 70%；加入 LiDAR 后，在 KITTI 和 VBR 上将 ATE 进一步降至亚米级。

Conclusion: AMB3R-SLAM 证明了无需静态世界假设的实时单目 SLAM 也能在公里级长序列和动态场景中保持高精度，并具备向多模态传感器扩展的潜力。

Abstract: We present AMB3R-SLAM, a real-time monocular SLAM system capable of reconstructing kilometer-scale trajectories over 10k frames on a single consumer-grade GPU. Our model couples a lightweight front-end for low-latency online tracking with a hierarchical backend that progressively enforces local, mid-level, and global consistency. By avoiding bundle adjustment that relies on the static world assumption, our system naturally handles complex dynamic scenes out of the box. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our method can be extended to leverage stereo, RGB-D, and LiDAR as additional inputs. AMB3R-SLAM achieves strong camera tracking performance across 9 datasets, reducing the absolute trajectory error (ATE) of previous state-of-the-art methods on VBR and Oxford Spires by over 70%. With additional LiDAR input, our model further reduces ATE to sub-meter level on KITTI and VBR datasets.

</details>


### [4] [ParticleSplat: Self-supervised Object-centric Latent Particle Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19463)
*Lyuxing He,Daniel Guo,Elizabeth Terveen,Deepak Pathak,David Held,Tal Daniel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ParticleSplat：一种自监督、以物体为中心的学习方法，通过前馈3D高斯泼溅将场景分解为表示语义实体的潜在“粒子”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Deep Latent Particles（DLP）等方法本质上是2D表示，缺乏显式3D空间与几何推理能力，而这对机器人操作等下游任务至关重要。

Method: 基于DLP，利用潜在粒子与3D高斯原语的结构相似性，引入3D潜在粒子空间，并以新视角合成目标训练；模型联合编码多视角图像与相机位姿到共享的3D物体中心潜在空间，再将粒子变换为粒子对齐的3D高斯，通过组合重建完整场景。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界数据集上，该方法无需监督即可学习物体掩码，支持可控3D场景编辑（如在潜空间修改粒子以移动物体），并提升机器人操作下游任务性能。

Conclusion: ParticleSplat将物体中心粒子表示扩展到3D高斯泼溅框架，实现了自监督3D场景分解、可控编辑以及面向机器人操作的表示学习增益。

Abstract: We present ParticleSplat, a self-supervised object-centric learning method that decomposes scenes into a set of latent ''particles'' representing semantic entities through feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting. Building on the Deep Latent Particles (DLP) framework, which represents images as a set of particles with attributes such as position, scale, and visual appearance, we address a key limitation of DLP: its inherently 2D nature, which prevents explicit 3D spatial and geometric reasoning that are critical for downstream tasks such as robotic manipulation. Leveraging the structural similarity between latent particles and 3D Gaussian primitives, we introduce a 3D latent particle space trained with a novel view synthesis objective. Our model jointly encodes multiple views with camera poses into a shared 3D object-centric latent space, then transforms particles into particle-aligned 3D Gaussians whose composition reconstructs the full scene. On simulated and real-world datasets, we show that this formulation inherently learns object masks without supervision and supports controllable 3D scene editing, such as moving objects by modifying particles in the latent space. We further establish that the learned 3D representation improves downstream performance on robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [5] [FAMOS: Feed-Forward 3D Articulation Modeling from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20817)
*Kevin Qu,Tao Sun,Massimiliano Viola,Liyuan Zhu,Zhizhuo Zhou,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Konrad Schindler,Iro Armeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAMOS 是一个前馈模型，可从稀疏无序的部分点云集合（含单视图）中预测可动部件分割与关节参数；它通过多状态关节 Transformer 聚合多观测证据，并利用观测关节跨度目标与程序化合成数据进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目稀疏视图只能提供部分几何与运动线索，而多数前馈方法仅从单次观测推断关节，严重依赖类别级形状先验，难以泛化到新类别或复杂运动。

Method: 输入稀疏、无序的部分点云集合，联合推理多个观测并支持可变输入数；提出 Multi-state Articulation Transformer，交替使用状态级注意力与全局注意力以聚合关节线索；设计观测关节跨度目标，监督各部件在输入观测中表现出的运动范围；并引入程序化数据生成器，在训练中合成带自标注的资产。

Result: 在 PartNet-Mobility、ACD 和 ArtiCraft-10K 上，FAMOS 相较前馈与基于优化的基线均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 联合多观测推理、运动跨度监督与程序化合成数据，可有效缓解稀疏单目视图下关节物体建模的几何与运动证据不足问题，并支持包括单视图在内的可变输入设置。

Abstract: Modeling articulated objects from sparse monocular views is challenging because each observation reveals only partial geometry and motion evidence. Most feed-forward methods infer articulation from a single observation and therefore rely heavily on learned category-level shape priors. We present FAMOS, a feed-forward model that predicts movable-part segmentation and joint parameters from a sparse, unordered set of partial point clouds. Our model jointly reasons over multiple observations and naturally supports a variable number of inputs, including a single view. To aggregate articulation cues across observations, we introduce a Multi-state Articulation Transformer with alternating state-wise and global attention. We further propose an observed articulation span objective that supervises the motion range each part exhibits across the input observations, encouraging the model to leverage the full observation set. To overcome the limited scale and diversity of existing datasets, we introduce a procedural data generator that synthesizes self-annotated assets during training. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility, ACD, and ArtiCraft-10K demonstrate consistent improvements over both feed-forward and optimization-based baselines. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [6] [FunArt: Decoding Functional Structure and Articulation from Generative 3D Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20673)
*Dennis Rotondi,Abdelrhman Werby,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunArt 从单次静态配置的 posed RGB-D 观测出发，构建 articulation-aware functional 3D scene graph；它利用 TRELLIS.2 的 O-Voxel 表示与冻结的稀疏压缩 VAE 作为结构先验，联合分割可动部件和功能交互元素，并估计运动学参数，在 Articulate3D 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人要在人类环境中有效工作，需要识别铰接物体、分割其可动与可交互部件，并估计运动学模型。现有铰接场景表示多依赖已观测交互来恢复运动学，而基于静态扫描的方法常将 articulation 与功能交互元素解耦，因此需要一种无需物理交互、能从静态观测初始化机器人感知与规划的方法。

Method: FunArt 重建物体实例，将其融合几何直接转换为 TRELLIS.2 的 O-Voxel 表示，并利用其冻结的稀疏压缩 VAE 作为结构先验。一个轻量 query-based decoder 结合紧凑的 object-level latents 与稠密的 surface-aligned features，联合分割 movable parts 和 functional interactive elements，同时估计 motion type、axis、origin 与 range。

Result: 在 Articulate3D 数据集上，FunArt 在 movable-part segmentation、articulation estimation 和 functional-element segmentation 上均达到 SOTA，且在提供或不提供 ground-truth object input 时均成立。端到端设置下，相较最强 baseline，movable parts 提升 1.5 AP_{50}，joint origin-and-axis constraints 提升 2.8 AP_{50}，functional elements 提升 6.7 AP_{50}。

Conclusion: 结果表明，生成式 3D latents 编码了可操作的结构线索，能够在物理交互之前初始化机器人感知与规划。

Abstract: To operate effectively in human environments, robots must identify articulated objects, segment their movable and interactive parts, and estimate their kinematic models. Existing articulated scene representations typically recover kinematics from observed interactions, while methods operating on static scans often decouple articulation from functional interactive elements. We present FunArt, a framework that constructs articulation-aware functional 3D scene graphs from posed RGB-D observations captured in a single static configuration. FunArt reconstructs object instances, converts their fused geometry directly into the O-Voxel representation of TRELLIS.2, and exploits its frozen, sparse-compression VAE as a structural prior. A lightweight query-based decoder combines compact object-level latents with dense, surface-aligned features to jointly segment movable parts and functional interactive elements while estimating motion type, axis, origin, and range. On the Articulate3D dataset, FunArt achieves state-of-the-art performance across movable-part segmentation, articulation estimation, and functional-element segmentation, both with and without ground-truth object input. In the end-to-end setting, it outperforms the strongest baselines by 1.5 AP_{50} points for movable parts, 2.8 AP_{50} points under joint origin-and-axis constraints, and 6.7 AP_{50} points for functional elements. These results demonstrate that generative 3D latents encode actionable structural cues that can initialize robotic perception and planning before physical interaction.

</details>


### [7] [SenseFuse: Label-Free Fusion of Image and Shape Encoders for Open-Vocabulary 3D Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20475)
*Euiseok Han,Tri Ton,Hwanhee Kim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseFuse 提出一种无标签的 2D 图像与 3D 形状编码器融合方法，仅改进现有开放词汇 3D 实例分割流程中的掩码标注阶段，在多个数据集上提升标注精度和实例 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇场景理解对机器人空间推理与物体操作至关重要；现有开放词汇 3D 实例分割方法在掩码标注时主要依赖 2D 图像特征或图像蒸馏表示，未能充分利用 3D 形状信息。

Method: 提出 SenseFuse：一种无标签融合方法，平衡 2D 图像编码器与 3D 形状编码器，仅细化现有流程的掩码标注阶段。基于 2D 与 3D 编码器失败模式高度不重叠、而两个 2D 编码器常犯相同错误的现象，引入自适应机制，利用单场景未标注候选在毫秒级估计无标签敏感性指标，从而选择场景级融合权重。

Result: 在 ScanNet200、Replica 和 ScanNet++ 上，SenseFuse 在所有评估设置中均提升标注精度，恢复 oracle 权重可获增益的 67–100%（中位数 93%），并在 22 个报告设置中的 21 个提升实例 AP。

Conclusion: SenseFuse 通过无标签融合 2D 图像与 3D 形状编码器，有效提升开放词汇 3D 实例分割的鲁棒性与标注性能，代码已公开。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary scene understanding is fundamental for robotics, laying the groundwork for spatial reasoning and object manipulation. While closed-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation heavily leverages 3D shape information, state-of-the-art open-vocabulary methods remain predominantly restricted to 2D image features or image-distilled representations during mask labeling. In this paper, we propose SenseFuse, a label-free fusion method that balances 2D image and 3D shape encoders for robust open-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation, refining only the mask-labeling stage of existing pipelines. We reveal that 2D image and 3D shape encoders exhibit largely disjoint failure patterns and rarely share identical wrong labels, whereas two 2D image encoders frequently repeat the same errors. This distinct behavior makes the 2D and 3D pair inherently complementary. We introduce an adaptive mechanism that selects a scene-level fusion weight to maximize a label-free sensitivity measure, estimated directly from a single scene's unlabeled proposals in milliseconds. SenseFuse improves labeling accuracy in every evaluated setting across ScanNet200, Replica, and ScanNet++, recovering 67-100% (median 93%) of the gain achievable with an oracle weight, and it raises instance AP in 21 of 22 reported settings. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [8] [EliGSiR: Continual RGB-D Mapping with Gaussian Splatting under Bounded Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20348)
*Björn Ellensohn,Elmar Rueckert,Christian Rauch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EliGSiR 是一种面向在线 RGB-D 连续建图的增量式高斯泼溅方法，通过证据引导与负载自适应机制，在保留已重建区域的同时高效利用优化预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 3D Gaussian Splatting 假设观测集合封闭且优化周期长；而连续 RGB-D 建图中新观测在线到达，且必须保留此前重建区域，因此需要控制可用优化预算如何随重建进程使用。

Method: 提出 EliGSiR（Evidence-guided Load-adaptive Incremental Gaussian Splatting with Image Replay）。其核心包括：Map-Guided View Scheduling 根据当前地图状态过滤冗余新视角并重新考虑保留视角；Load-Adaptive Fidelity 依据当前建图负载调整监督分辨率，而非固定分辨率调度；Targeted Geometry Growth 将深度监督与高斯创建分离，仅在重复 RGB-D 观测表明结构缺失或错位处增加几何容量。三者共同自适应决定优化哪些视角、使用多少图像细节以及表征在何处增长，并在建图持续进行时生效。

Result: 在 Replica、TUM RGB-D、ScanNet++ 和真实 RGB-D 传感器序列上评估，同时考虑最终重建与采集过程中的地图表现。在 TUM RGB-D fr3/long_office_household 上，使用与受控基线相同的真值建图位姿时，EliGSiR 达到 21.52 dB，优于 SplaTAM 的 19.42 dB；在跟踪位姿比较中，EliGSiR 结合实时 ORB-SLAM3 位姿达到 23.02 dB，耗时 155.5 s，优于使用原生跟踪器的 CaRtGS 的 20.10 dB 与 230.9 s。

Conclusion: EliGSiR 通过自适应视角调度、监督保真度和几何增长，提高了连续 RGB-D 建图的可用预算利用效率，并在重建质量和效率上优于现有基线。

Abstract: Conventional 3D Gaussian Splatting assumes a closed set of observations and long optimization schedules. Continual RGB-D mapping in contrast poses the problem that new observations arrive online, while previously reconstructed regions must be preserved. We present EliGSiR (Evidence-guided Load-adaptive Incremental Gaussian Splatting with Image Replay), a continual Gaussian mapper that controls how the available optimization budget is used as the reconstruction evolves. Map-Guided View Scheduling filters redundant incoming views and reconsiders retained views according to the current state of the map. Load-Adaptive Fidelity adjusts supervision resolution to the current mapping load instead of following a fixed resolution schedule. Targeted Geometry Growth separates depth supervision from Gaussian creation and adds geometric capacity only where repeated RGB-D observations indicate missing or misplaced structure. Together, these mechanisms adapt which views are optimized, how much image detail is used, and where the representation grows while mapping remains active. We evaluate EliGSiR on Replica, TUM RGB-D, ScanNet++, and real RGB-D sensor sequences, considering both the final reconstruction and the map available throughout acquisition. On TUM RGB-D fr3/long_office_household, EliGSiR reaches 21.52 dB with the same ground-truth mapping poses used by the controlled baselines, compared with 19.42 dB for SplaTAM. In the tracked-pose comparison, EliGSiR with live ORB-SLAM3 poses reaches 23.02 dB in 155.5 s, compared with 20.10 dB in 230.9 s for CaRtGS using its native tracker. We further evaluate reconstruction throughout acquisition and show how EliGSiR adaptive view scheduling, supervision fidelity, and geometry growth improve the use of the available mapping budget.

</details>


### [9] [Scene-Q: Confidence-Aware Coarse-to-Fine Querying of 3D Scenes with Selective VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20235)
*Juno Kim,Yesol Park,Hye-Jung Yoon,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Scene-Q：一种置信度感知的由粗到细查询框架，通过温度缩放归一化编码器分数，并仅在低置信度时调用推理 VLM，以低成本提升开放词汇 3D 场景理解中的查询 grounding 与消歧能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内移动机器人需要开放词汇场景理解，将自然语言查询 grounding 到一致的 3D 地图中。现有系统多依赖对比图像-文本编码器与余弦相似度检索，虽然高效，但当标签近义或存在多个相似实例时表现脆弱。

Method: Scene-Q 先对编码器分数进行温度缩放归一化，形成置信度感知机制：高置信度查询走快速检索路径；低置信度或歧义查询则在小规模 top-K 候选集上，利用原始多视图图像与实例边界框进行重排，并选择性调用推理 VLM，实现上下文感知消歧。

Result: 在 ScanNet200 开放词汇 3D 实例分割和真实世界重建上的自然语言 3D 实例检索任务中取得提升，空间与关系型查询增益最大，同时相当一部分查询仍保持在快速路径上。

Conclusion: Scene-Q 通过选择性 VLM 推理与多视图候选重排，在控制成本的同时增强开放词汇 3D 实例分割与自然语言 3D 实例检索，尤其适合需一致 3D 地图 grounding 的室内移动机器人场景理解。

Abstract: Indoor mobile robots require open-vocabulary scene understanding that grounds natural-language queries in a consistent 3D map. Many existing systems ultimately rely on cosine-similarity retrieval with contrastive image--text encoders, which is efficient but brittle when labels are near-synonymous or multiple similar instances appear. We present Scene-Q, a confidence-aware coarse-to-fine querying framework that normalizes encoder scores with temperature scaling and selectively invokes a reasoning VLM only for low-confidence cases. High-confidence queries are answered by fast retrieval, while ambiguous ones are reranked over a small top-K candidate set using the original multi-view images and instance bounding boxes, enabling context-aware disambiguation at low cost. Scene-Q improves open-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation on ScanNet200 and natural-language 3D instance retrieval on real-world reconstructions, with the largest gains on spatial and relational queries while keeping a substantial fraction of queries on the fast path.

</details>


### [10] [Astronex-World 1.0: Real-Time Interactive World Model Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20034)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Astronex-World 1.0，一个开放、可控的视频世界模型基础模型：支持文本/图像到视频，在帧对齐相机轨迹、连续动作、具身标识及指定位置文本事件条件下预测未来视觉状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建开放且可控的视频世界模型基础，统一支持相机、动作、具身与事件控制，并实现持久、实时的视频生成；同时降低训练与推理资源门槛。

Method: 基于 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B 先验，包含双向全上下文生成模型与采用块因果注意力及跨块 KV 缓存的因果模型；PRoPE 注入相机内参和外参，64 维动作流调制每个 Transformer 层；五阶段训练：双向相机与动作控制、骨干转为块因果生成、少步学生蒸馏、混合域动态恢复、非对称 DMD/DMD2 分布匹配。

Result: 因果模型以 832x480、24 fps 生成视频；五阶段训练在两张 NVIDIA L20 48 GB GPU 上完成，因果模型可在单卡实时流式生成；WBench Navi 得分 73.5，WBench Full 得分 70.0；在 Full 上，该 5B 模型优于 13.6B LongCat-Video 和 14B Helios，与 22B LTX-2.3 相差 1 分以内，并优于在 NVIDIA A100 上基于同一 5B 先验后训练的 YUME 1.5。

Conclusion: Astronex-World 1.0 是开放可控的视频世界模型基础，保留动作输入与输出接口，可后训练用于具身智能和自动驾驶。

Abstract: We present Astronex-World 1.0, an open controllable video world-model foundation. Given a text prompt (text-to-video) or an initial observation (image-to-video), the model predicts future visual states under frame-aligned camera trajectories, continuous actions, and an embodiment identifier, and accepts text events inserted at a specified position of a rollout. The family provides a bidirectional model for full-context generation and a causal model with block-causal attention and cross-block KV caching for persistent generation, both built on the Wan2.2-TI2V-5B prior. PRoPE injects camera intrinsics and extrinsics, while a 64-dimensional action stream modulates every Transformer layer. A five-stage training path develops bidirectional camera and action control, converts the backbone to block-causal generation, distills a few-step student, restores mixed-domain dynamics, and applies asymmetric DMD/DMD2 distribution matching. The causal model generates 832x480 video at 24 fps. All five training stages run on two NVIDIA L20 48 GB GPUs, and the causal model streams in real time on one. It scores 73.5 on WBench Navi and 70.0 on WBench Full. On Full, this 5B model is above the 13.6B LongCat-Video and the 14B Helios, within one point of the 22B LTX-2.3, and above YUME 1.5, which is post-trained from the same 5B prior on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. The reserved action input and output interfaces allow post-training for embodied intelligence and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Patch Removal: Persistent Adversarial Effects in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19669)
*Enhao Wu,Fusen Guo,Yuxin Cao,Ziyang Lyu,Lin Li,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究揭示VLA策略的对抗补丁会造成即时动作破坏，并在补丁移除后留下持续状态效应；作者提出状态恢复协议和恢复适配器来量化可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多关注持续攻击，未区分即时动作破坏与补丁移除后的持续状态影响，也未控制遮挡、动作误差幅度和方向持续性等混杂因素。

Method: 提出状态恢复协议：在匹配的动作块边界移除补丁，并在相同剩余步数预算下测量后续可恢复性；使用干净、随机补丁、偏差匹配和固定方向对照，以区分对抗效应与遮挡、动作误差幅度和方向持续性。同时评估在攻击诱导状态上训练、并受控干预延迟影响的恢复适配器。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT与EDPA攻击下，五个动作块后仅36.2%的LIBERO-Long回合可恢复，而偏差匹配和固定方向对照分别为89.9%和87.0%；自回归OpenVLA也观察到类似持续效应。恢复适配器在一块延迟下将恢复率从7.7%提升至47.4%，但延迟干预时收益显著下降。

Conclusion: 对抗补丁的效应可在补丁移除后持续存在，及时干预对策略恢复至关重要。

Abstract: Adversarial patches to Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies can cause both immediate action corruption and persistent state effects that remain after the patch is removed. Existing evaluations largely focus on continuous attacks and do not separate these two effects. We introduce a state-restoration protocol that removes the patch at matched action-chunk boundaries and measures subsequent recoverability under the same remaining step budget. Clean, random-patch, deviation-matched, and fixed-direction controls distinguish adversarial effects from occlusion, action-error magnitude, and directional persistence. We also evaluate a recovery adapter trained on attack-induced states under controlled intervention latency. On OpenVLA-OFT with EDPA attacks, only 36.2% of LIBERO-Long episodes remain recoverable after five chunks, compared with 89.9% and 87.0% for the deviation-matched and fixed-direction controls. Similar persistent effects are observed on autoregressive OpenVLA. The recovery adapter improves recovery from 7.7% to 47.4% at one-chunk latency, but its benefit decreases substantially with delayed intervention. These results show that adversarial effects can persist after patch removal and that timely intervention is critical for recovery.

</details>

### [12] ["Your Robot Was Trained on a Lie": Collision Mesh Poisoning Attacks on Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18122)
*Gengyang Xu,Dongwei Xiao,Yiteng Peng,Yanbo Dai,Ruochen Zhou,Shing-Chi Cheung,Xiaoyu Ji,Wenyuan Xu,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出碰撞网格投毒（CMP）：利用仿真3D资产中视觉网格与碰撞网格可合法不一致的视觉-碰撞间隙（V-C Gap），攻击者仅篡改碰撞网格并经资产供应链分发，使机器人操作策略在仿真中表现正常，却在真实部署中退化、失败或引发物理安全风险，且现有防御不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略日益依赖仿真器进行训练与评估，而仿真3D资产通常包含用于渲染的视觉网格和用于物理交互的碰撞网格。为计算效率，碰撞网格常被粗粒度近似，与视觉网格不必一致，形成普遍且合理的V-C Gap。当前资产审查主要覆盖恶意软件、版权与格式合规，不检查视觉-碰撞一致性，因此V-C Gap构成新的实际攻击面。

Method: 提出碰撞网格投毒（CMP），这是首个通过3D资产供应链针对机器人操作的投毒攻击。攻击者仅修改3D资产的碰撞网格，保持视觉网格及其他组件不变。含毒资产可经合法供应链渠道传播。策略在仿真中训练和评估时行为正常，但部署到真实世界后因视觉与碰撞几何不一致而出现性能退化、任务失败或物理安全风险。作者还评估了若干现有防御。

Result: 实验表明，使用含毒资产训练和评估的策略在仿真中表现正常，但在真实世界中会退化、失败或造成物理安全风险。对多种防御的评估显示，现有防御不足以抵御CMP。

Conclusion: V-C Gap是机器人学习供应链中一个被忽视且实用的新攻击面；CMP证明仅篡改碰撞网格即可实现隐蔽且有效的投毒攻击。需要新的防御机制来保障视觉-碰撞一致性，并加强3D资产在机器人操作训练与部署中的安全审查。

Abstract: Learning-enabled robotic manipulation increasingly relies on robot simulators for policy training and evaluation before real-world deployment. Inside a simulator, a 3D asset contains two separate geometries: a visual mesh used for rendering and a collision mesh used for physical interaction. For computational efficiency, the collision mesh is deliberately a coarse approximation that need not have the same geometry as the visual mesh, a legitimate and pervasive discrepancy we call the Visual--Collision Gap (V--C Gap). We show that the V--C Gap opens a new and practical attack surface, and propose Collision Mesh Poisoning (CMP), the first poisoning attack against robotic manipulation delivered through the 3D asset supply chain. An attacker modifies only the collision mesh of a 3D asset, leaving the visual mesh and all other components unchanged. A policy trained and evaluated with the poisoned asset behaves normally throughout simulation, yet degrades, fails, or creates physical safety risks once deployed in the real world. Since current asset review practices cover malware, copyright, and format compliance, but not visual--collision consistency, poisoned assets can be distributed through legitimate supply chain channels. We evaluate several defenses and our results show that they are insufficient to defend against CMP, highlighting the need for new defenses.

</details>

### [13] [From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19630)
*Diba Afroze,Xingli Zhang,Yazhou Tu,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出面向车载语音助手LLM预执行授权决策的202场景基准与七类分类法，评估本地与API模型，发现即使最佳模型对齐率达89.1%仍存在误执行，结构化LLM决策不能单独作为安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被集成到车载语音助手，但将自然语言请求映射到车辆功能会带来安全关键的授权问题；系统执行前必须决定执行、拒绝、澄清、要求确认、转手动、触发紧急响应或不调用工具。既有评估未在说话人角色、认证状态、车辆状态和工具可用性条件下隔离这一预动作决策。

Method: 构建包含202个场景、带七类参考决策的基准；评估2个本地开放权重模型和3个API LLM，使用决策对齐率与安全专用错误指标；对Llama 3.2 3B进行受控消融，比较结构化授权策略与仅schema、通用安全基线。

Result: 对齐率从Llama 3.2 3B的40.1%到Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview的89.1%；API模型为83.2%-89.1%，彼此无统计显著差异；即使这些模型在161个非执行场景中仍产生2-3次False Execute，确认和手动控制决策持续出错；消融中结构化授权策略将Llama 3.2 3B对齐率提高到40.1%，高于仅schema和通用安全基线的28.2%-29.2%，但未消除False Execute。

Conclusion: 结构化LLM决策不足以作为独立安全机制；部署时需要独立执行层，在调用任何车辆功能前验证工具权限与车辆状态约束。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes. Structured LLM decisions are therefore insufficient as a standalone safety mechanism, and deployment requires an independent enforcement layer that verifies tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints before invoking any vehicle function.

</details>


### [14] [JEPA-WAM: Connecting Generated Visual Instructions to World Action Models through JEPA Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20277)
*Tianbin Liu,Jian Zhu,Taiyi Su,Jianjun Zhang,Chong Ma,Zitai Huang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 JEPA-WAM，通过为文本指令生成多样化视觉目标参考，并用冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 编码为紧凑目标 token，提升世界动作模型的指令跟随能力与泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）虽在机器人操作中表现强，但仅依赖文本指令时指令跟随能力有限。作者认为原因之一是机器人学习数据存在结构性失衡：视觉-动作轨迹丰富，而语言标注稀疏且重复，使策略更依赖视觉上下文和运动规律来识别任务，而非真正 grounding 文本指令。

Method: JEPA-WAM 为每条文本指令引入一组随机生成的视觉指令：使用现成文本到图像生成器，在不训练生成器的情况下采样多张任务完成图像。尽管这些图像在外观和布局上可能与当前场景不同，但语义上与指令对齐，可作为视觉目标参考。随后用冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 编码器提取任务级语义，并将稠密目标表示压缩为紧凑 goal tokens，通过 cross-attention 同时条件化视频专家和动作专家。此外，构建了覆盖分布内、分布外场景和分布外指令的真实机器人指令跟随基准。

Result: 在该基准上，JEPA-WAM 在分布内、OOD 场景和 OOD 指令三种设定下分别达到 87.3%、74.5% 和 80.9% 的成功率；相比 π0 与 Fast-WAM，至少分别高出 10.0、27.3 和 14.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过生成多样化视觉目标参考并结合 JEPA 语义表示，可有效缓解机器人数据中语言监督稀疏的问题，增强文本指令 grounding 与分布外泛化能力；所构建的真实机器人基准也为指令跟随评估提供了测试平台。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated strong robotic manipulation capabilities by augmenting pretrained video generative models with action experts. However, current WAMs still show limited instruction-following ability when conditioned solely on text instructions. We argue that this limitation stems in part from a structural imbalance in robot-learning data: rich visual-action trajectories are often paired with sparse and repetitive language annotations, allowing policies to identify tasks from visual context and motion regularities rather than grounding the instruction itself. To address this limitation, we introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments each text instruction with a bank of stochastically generated visual instructions, providing diverse visual cues for instruction following. Specifically, JEPA-WAM uses an off-the-shelf text-to-image generator to sample multiple task-completion images conditioned on the text instruction, without training the generator. Although these generated images may differ from the current visual scene in appearance and layout, they remain semantically aligned with the instruction and serve as visual goal references. To focus on task-level semantics beyond appearance, we encode these references with a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder. The resulting dense goal representations are compressed into compact goal tokens that condition both the video and action experts through cross-attention. We further construct a real-robot instruction-following benchmark covering in-distribution, out-of-distribution scene, and out-of-distribution instruction settings. On this benchmark, JEPA-WAM achieves success rates of 87.3%, 74.5%, and 80.9% in these three settings, outperforming {\pi}0 and Fast-WAM by at least 10.0, 27.3, and 14.5 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [15] [Constraint-Safe Graph-Context Scoring for Stable Point-Feature Labels Under Text-Width and Accessibility-Inspired Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19848)
*Taimoor Ahmad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LABELSENSE-Pilot，一个面向交互地图点要素标注的可复现原型：为每个要素生成8个罗盘候选，用MLP基于图上下文摘要评分，加入上一布局奖励，并通过混合整数优化选布局；实验在2,500个机场点上验证显示率、闪烁、碰撞及放大字体下的几何违规，定位为可审计工程权衡而非人类可用性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互地图点标注需同时兼顾几何有效、显示产出、局部放置效用与相机运动稳定性；无障碍与多语言会改变标签尺寸，但现有算法评估常简化为重叠计数，忽略上述多维约束与稳定性。

Method: 方法为每个点要素生成8个罗盘方向候选；以多层感知机对图上下文摘要进行候选评分，并加入上一放置奖励；通过混合整数优化选择最终布局。评分器明确不称为图Transformer。所有返回布局均检查视口包含、逐要素唯一性与两两间距。实验使用来自155个国家的2,500个机场坐标与名称，采用按国家分组划分，并生成密度、相机、文本后缀、偏好和放大字体等压力条件。

Result: 五随机种子下，LABELSENSE-Pilot显示85.62%标签，闪烁2.09%，碰撞为零。相比手工效用整数规划，显示率牺牲1.43个百分点，但闪烁降低12.04个百分点。感知放大框的布局零代理违规；而标准几何在1.5倍重新评估时，52.57%的已选放置违规。

Conclusion: 结果证明了一种可审计的工程权衡，而非人类无障碍、多语言可用性或偏好证据；投稿前仍需官方近期基线与参与者证据。

Abstract: Point-feature label placement on interactive maps must reconcile geometric validity, display yield, local placement utility, and stability across camera motion. Accessibility and multilingual requirements further change label dimensions, yet algorithmic evaluations often collapse these concerns into overlap counts. We present LABELSENSE-Pilot, a reproducible prototype that generates eight compass candidates per feature, scores candidates with a multilayer perceptron over graph-context summaries, adds a previous-placement bonus, and selects a layout through mixed-integer optimization. The executed scorer is deliberately not described as a graph transformer. Every returned layout is checked for viewport containment, per-feature uniqueness, and pairwise clearance. Experiments use 2,500 airport coordinates and names spanning 155 countries, with country-grouped splits and generated density, camera, text-suffix, preference, and enlarged-font stressors. Across five seeds, LABELSENSE-Pilot displayed 85.62 percent of labels with 2.09 percent flicker and zero collisions. Versus a handcrafted-utility integer program, LABELSENSE-Pilot sacrificed 1.43 percentage points of display while reducing flicker by 12.04 points. Enlarged-box-aware layouts produced zero proxy violations, whereas standard geometry reevaluated at 1.5x violated 52.57 percent of selected placements. These results establish an auditable engineering trade-off, not human accessibility, multilingual usability, or preference. Official recent baselines and participant evidence remain required before submission.

</details>

### [16] [Feasibility and Singularity in High-Order Safety-Critical Control for Quadrotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19362)
*Omayra Yago Nieto,Leonardo Colombo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对有界输入下四旋翼集群的高阶安全临界控制，提出力矩感知动态扩展与高斯过程学习四阶HOCBF残差的QP方法，在假设下保证成对避碰并恢复名义输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼编队避碰中，平方距离障碍在相对位移与可用推力方向正交时会削弱推力有效性；同时，非奇异约束在共享输入界下仍可能联合不可行。因此需要同时处理高阶动力学、有界执行器、未知扰动与成对避碰可行性问题。

Method: 定义成对有效性度量与聚合可行性度量来刻画上述两类现象；引入力矩感知动态扩展，将姿态力矩显式暴露于四阶障碍函数中，避免正推力下扩展输入行消失；利用高斯过程直接学习四阶HOCBF残差，无需对未知扰动求导即可获得鲁棒裕度；最终构造二次规划控制器。

Result: 在残差有界与持续可行性假设下，所得QP保证成对碰撞避免；当名义输入满足鲁棒安全与执行器约束时，控制器可恢复名义输入。同时给出了推力有效性与共享界下联合可行性的量化刻画。

Conclusion: 该方法将可行性度量、力矩感知高阶障碍函数与高斯过程残差学习结合，为有界输入下四旋翼团队安全临界控制提供了理论保证，并兼顾安全性与名义性能恢复。

Abstract: We study high-order safety-critical control of quadrotor teams under bounded inputs and pairwise collision-avoidance constraints. Squared-distance barriers may lose thrust effectiveness when the relative displacement is orthogonal to the available thrust directions, while nonsingular constraints may still be jointly infeasible under shared bounds. We characterize both phenomena through pairwise effectiveness and aggregate feasibility measures. A torque-aware dynamic extension exposes attitude torques in a fourth-order barrier and prevents the extended-input row from vanishing under positive thrust. Gaussian processes directly learn the fourth-order HOCBF residual, providing robust margins without differentiating unknown perturbations. Under residual-bound and persistent-feasibility assumptions, the resulting QP guarantees collision avoidance and recovers the nominal input whenever it satisfies the robust safety and actuator constraints.

</details>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [17] [S4R: Scaling for Rigid-Body Interpenetration Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20524)
*Zhiyang Dou,Ang Zhao,Chen Peng,Minghao Guo,Haixu Wu,Cheng Lin,Yuan Liu,Junfeng Yao,Xiaohu Guo,Wenping Wang,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: S4R是一种基于尺度延拓的静态刚体互穿修复方法：先整体缩小到无穿透尺度，再用一系列最小范数凸接触QP逐步恢复原尺度，从而把深穿透修正转化为浅接触子问题，并在Kubric、HY3D-Bench和Thingi10K上实现零穿透、低位移和最低墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化组装与生成的场景中经常出现刚体互穿，必须在物理仿真等下游应用前消除；静态互穿修复，尤其在大规模场景中，仍具挑战。

Method: S4R先将每个刚体绕固定参考中心均匀缩小至无穿透的初始尺度，再通过连续的最小范数凸接触二次规划（QP）序列恢复全尺度，并以线性化分离裕度为目标。该方法用保守的尺度事件界和冻结见证间隙预测减少精确网格查询，最后进行全尺度评估器检查与有界尾部细化，并提供GPU实现以扩展至大规模场景。

Result: 在Kubric、HY3D-Bench和Thingi10K上，使用共享网格级评估器和统一逐场景计时协议，最多N=5000个刚体时，S4R在所有三个基准的主要对比中均达到零报告穿透，位移小且几乎不随场景规模变化，并在各硬件层级中取得最低墙钟时间；GPU实现进一步扩展到大规模场景。

Conclusion: S4R能有效且可扩展地修复刚体互穿，在多个基准和大规模场景中优于对比方法；代码与数据已在项目页面公开。

Abstract: Rigid-body interpenetration frequently occurs in procedurally assembled and generated scenes and must be removed before downstream applications such as physical simulation. We present S4R (Scaling for Rigid-Body Interpenetration Resolution), a scale-continuation method for static interpenetration repair. S4R first uniformly shrinks each body about a fixed reference center to a small initial scale, at which the layout is penetration-free, and then restores full scale through a sequence of minimum-norm convex contact quadratic programs (QPs) that target the linearized separation margin during continuation. Resolution thereby replaces one deep correction with a sequence of shallow-contact subproblems. A conservative scale-event bound and frozen-witness gap predictions cut the number of exact mesh queries; the continuation then ends with a full-scale evaluator check and bounded tail refinement. We evaluate S4R on Kubric, HY3D-Bench, and Thingi10K using a shared mesh-level evaluator and a unified per-scene timing protocol. In the main comparisons on all three benchmarks, up to N=5000 bodies, S4R reaches zero reported penetration with displacement that stays small and nearly independent of scene size, and at the lowest wall time within each hardware tier among the compared methods. A GPU implementation extends these results to large-scale scenes. Our code and data can be found on our project page: this https URL .

</details>

### [18] [AthenaZero: A low-inertia, bimanual robot for dynamic manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19194)
*Andrew S. Morgan,Gregory Xie,Capprin Bass,Rachel Thomasson,Chunpeng Wang,Erfan Shahriari,Harrison Busa,Oluwaseun Araromi,Joseph Aronov,Michael Burgess,Velin D. Dimitrov,Matthew A. Estrada,Faris Hamdi,Samuel Kendig,Taeyoon Lee,Jose Oscar Mur-Miranda,Emma Sommers,Paul Titchener,Margaret Wang,Achu Wilson,Mark Yeatman,Osman Dogan Yirmibesoglu,Alfred A. Rizzi,Annan Mozeika,Nicolas Rojas,Lael Odhner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AthenaZero双臂机械臂通过准直驱驱动和传动远程化实现低惯量，末端质量接近人类，适用于动态操作；在抛掷、接球、击球等棒球任务中表现出高速性能（抛掷>30 m/s，接/击>14 m/s）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械臂惯量大，限制动态操作；需要在不牺牲控制权限的前提下降低惯性，实现与人类相当时间尺度上的复杂交互。

Method: 采用准直驱驱动和传动远程化技术设计双臂机械臂，实现低有效末端质量和力矩透明性，并描述设计方法。

Result: 抛掷速度超30 m/s，接球和击球速度超14 m/s（7.3米距离）；完成机器人-机器人和人-机器人投接练习与击球练习，展示高效性和适应性。

Conclusion: AthenaZero适合高速动态操作，能在毫秒级时间尺度上完成复杂交互，验证了低惯量设计的有效性。

Abstract: AthenaZero is a bimanual manipulator designed to minimize inertia without compromising control authority. By utilizing quasi-direct drive actuation and transmission remotization techniques, the system achieves an effective endpoint mass comparable to that of a human---about an order of magnitude less than conventional robot manipulators. This characteristic, combined with its inherent torque transparency, makes AthenaZero exceptionally well-suited for dynamic manipulation. We describe the methodology} that led to this design and demonstrate the robot's capabilities on three baseball-inspired tasks: throwing, catching, and batting, which showcase complex interactions on human-comparable timescales where milliseconds matter. AthenaZero was capable of throwing at speeds in excess of 30 m/s, with catching and batting at speeds in excess of 14 m/s over a short 7.3 m distance. Batting practice and a game of catch were subsequently performed in robot-to-robot and human-to-robot variations, showcasing the efficacy and adaptability of our system in tasks that require high acceleration.

</details>


### [19] [Coding Agents with an Obstacle-Aware Harness for Safe Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20822)
*Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang,Zhen Dong,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文评估编码智能体在机器人操作中的安全性，发现其常为完成目标而碰撞障碍物，根源在规划未将安全约束作为优先项；提出SafeHarness，通过障碍物感知的路径规划与接触执行提升安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体已能无需机器人专用代码操作机器人，但其在安全约束下的表现未被研究。任务同时给出操作目标与不可触碰障碍物，需考察智能体是否兼顾任务与安全。

Method: 将操作分解为路径阶段与富接触阶段，定位失败来源：路径阶段缺乏避障路线与不可行时重规划，接触阶段未以同一约束限制接触执行。SafeHarness提供两个障碍物感知工具：路径规划将物体表示为边界框并生成路点候选路线，使智能体预先规划、验证、必要时重规划再执行；接触执行选择使接触本身避开障碍物的接触位置。

Result: SafeHarness取得71.9%任务成功率和87.5%避碰率，较此前SOTA分别提升6.5%和27.0%，为同一智能体无工具版本的2.3倍和1.5倍。

Conclusion: 编码智能体安全失败主要源于规划未优先考虑安全约束；通过障碍物感知的路径与接触harness可显著提升安全性与任务成功率。

Abstract: Coding agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation: a language model writes the robot controller as a program, and agents built in this way now operate robots without robot-specific this http URL this paradigm is also safe, however, has not been asked. We evaluate coding agent under a safety constraint, where each task pairs a manipulation goal with an obstacle the robot must not touch. The agent pursues the goal but collides with the obstacle in most cases, treating task completion as its sole objective while neglecting safety. The agent reasons about the obstacle in its traces, and the prompt already forbids touching it, so neither perception nor instruction is at fault; the fault lies in the planning, where the stated constraint never becomes a priority. By decomposing manipulation into a route phase and a contact-rich moment, we locate the source of the failure. Along the route, the model cannot prioritize the safety constraint, having no notion of a clearing route and none of replanning once a chosen route becomes infeasible. At the contact, it is unaware that contact execution is bounded by the same constraint. To close this gap, we present SafeHarness, which equips the model with two obstacle-aware harnesses that enable it to prioritize the safety constraint. Obstacle-aware route planning grounds the objects as bounding boxes and draws candidate routes over them as sequences of waypoints. The agent then plans a route in advance, verifies it, replans when necessary, and only then executes it. Obstacle-aware contact execution instead selects the contact position so that the contact itself avoids the obstacle. SafeHarness attains 71.9% task success and 87.5% collision avoidance, surpassing the previous SOTA by 6.5% and 27.0%, respectively. These results are $2.3\times$ and $1.5\times$ those of the same agent without harnesses.

</details>


### [20] [Workspace Models: Lightweight Robotic Memory via Saliency-Driven Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20820)
*Nitish Dashora,Douglas Chen,Idan Shenfeld,John Marangola,Pulkit Agrawal,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 workspace token：一种训练时利用 VLM 蒸馏得到的轻量级潜在记忆表示，部署时可替代观测，使策略无需在线 VLM 推理即可解决记忆密集型机器人操作任务，且性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂机器人操作常需长期记忆，但直接条件于完整历史会使策略易受虚假相关影响并降低性能；现有方法依赖在线 VLM 查询压缩历史信息，计算开销大。

Method: 训练时用 VLM 识别完成任务所需的当前与历史信息，并通过集合重建解码器损失将其蒸馏为 workspace token；部署时以该 token 作为观测的即插即用替代。

Result: 在仿真和硬件中，workspace token 使策略无需在线 VLM 推理即可完成记忆密集型任务；相比基线更轻量，且策略性能更好。

Conclusion: workspace token 是一种高效有效的记忆表示，可在部署时替代昂贵的 VLM 在线推理，并提升策略在长期记忆任务中的表现。

Abstract: Complex robotic manipulation tasks frequently require a long-term memory of past events and actions. As conditioning on full histories renders policies prone to spurious correlations and degrades performance, many approaches to policy memory involve compressing historical information through expensive VLM queries in-the-loop to process only task-salient information. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach in which computationally intensive VLM queries are made during train-time to learn a lightweight latent memory that can be efficiently queried at deployment time. Our representation, which we call the \textbf{workspace token}, is trained by (1) using a VLM to identify current and historical information necessary for completing a task, then (2) distilling these into the workspace token using a set-reconstruction decoder loss. In both simulation and hardware, we show that the workspace token can be used as a drop-in replacement for observations during deployment, enabling policies to solve memory-intensive tasks without the need for VLM reasoning in-the-loop. Interestingly, we found that workspace tokens are not only more lightweight but also lead to better policy performance.

</details>


### [21] [StageGuard: Learning Stage Transitions for Long-Horizon Robot Tasks via Agentic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20791)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Yangzheng Wu,Tengyue Ba,Zhanguang Zhang,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StageGuard，一种面向分层机器人规划中阶段转换决策的智能体蒸馏框架，通过教师模型推理与示范轨迹蒸馏轻量学生 VLM，实现准确、高效的子任务完成判断与技能切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层规划需组合多个机器人控制策略完成长时程任务，关键是在合适时机终止当前技能并进入下一子任务；现有方法依赖难以在真实执行中获得的预设计完成信号检查器，而大规模 VLM 虽具推理能力，但其决策边界与任务完成标准不一致，且云端部署与长推理带来高延迟，限制实时监控。

Method: StageGuard 结合教师模型推理与示范轨迹，生成关于子任务完成和策略切换的结构化解释；轻量学生 VLM 基于这些解释生成紧凑自解释，并通过监督微调学习阶段转换决策，以支持在线监测。

Result: 在两个基准的轨迹上评估阶段转换预测，并在 BEHAVIOR-1K 上集成到分层机器人控制中评估闭环任务成功率，同时在真实机器人上进一步验证；结果表明阶段转换预测显著提升，并能支持高效在线监控。

Conclusion: StageGuard 能实现准确且高效的阶段转换决策，为长时程分层机器人任务执行中的实时技能终止与切换提供可行方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical planning frameworks combine skills from multiple robot control policies for long-horizon task execution, where determining when to terminate the current skill and advance to the next subtask is essential. Existing approaches often rely on pre-designed completion signal checkers that are hard to obtain in real-world execution. Large-scale vision-language models (VLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities, but their decision boundaries are not inherently aligned with task completion criteria, while cloud deployment and lengthy reasoning introduce substantial latency, limiting real-time monitoring. We propose StageGuard, an agentic distillation framework for accurate and efficient stage-transition decisions. StageGuard combines teacher-model reasoning with demonstration trajectories to generate structured explanations of subtask completion and policy switching. A lightweight student VLM uses these explanations to generate compact self-explanations, which are used for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate stage-transition prediction on trajectories from two benchmarks and assess closed-loop task success through integration into hierarchical robot control on BEHAVIOR-1K, with further validation on real robots. Results show substantial improvements in stage-transition prediction while supporting efficient online monitoring.

</details>


### [22] [GeoAAC: Geometry-Based Adaptive Action Chunking from Denoising Trajectories in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20776)
*Xin Chen,Sen Chen,Yujuan Ding,Jian Liu,Guoqing Wang,Wei Ye,Heng Tao Shen,Yi Bin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GeoAAC：一种面向流匹配（Flow Matching）VLA 策略的基于几何的自适应动作分块方法，可根据当前动作预测可靠性动态调整动作视野，无需额外训练，并在多种仿真与真实操作任务中优于固定视野基线和现有自适应方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略在动作生成与执行中广泛采用动作分块，但通常使用固定动作视野。实际 rollout 中不同任务阶段对动作连续性、控制精度和闭环反馈的需求不同，固定视野难以适应变化的控制需求。

Method: GeoAAC 利用 Flow Matching 去噪轨迹的几何信息作为过程级预测可靠性信号，发现动作前缀间的几何变化与预测不确定性正相关。基于前缀级几何构建视野级几何轮廓，从而在单次生成中自适应确定动作视野，无需额外训练。

Result: 在 GR00T N1.5 和 pi0.5 上，于 LIBERO、LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa365 及真实世界操作任务中验证。相比固定动作视野基线和现有自适应方法，取得一致提升：仿真最高提升 8.7 个百分点；真实世界平均成功率从 53.3% 提升至 74.4%。

Conclusion: 几何信息可作为过程级可靠性指标，用于无需训练的自适应动作分块，使 VLA 策略能根据任务阶段动态平衡动作连续性与控制精度，从而提升仿真和真实场景下的控制性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Action chunking is widely used for action generation and execution in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, yet existing approaches commonly use a fixed action horizon. During a rollout, different task stages may require different levels of action continuity, control precision, and closed-loop feedback, making a fixed horizon unable to accommodate changing control requirements. We propose \textbf{GeoAAC}, a geometry-based adaptive action chunking method for flow-based VLA policies that adjusts the action horizon according to the reliability of the current action prediction. We show that the geometry of Flow Matching denoising trajectories provides process-level information for characterizing prediction reliability, with geometric variation across action prefixes remaining positively correlated with predictive uncertainty. GeoAAC uses this prefix-wise geometry to construct a horizon-wise geometric profile and adaptively determine the action horizon from a single generation without additional training. Experiments with GR00T N1.5 and {\pi}0.5 on LIBERO, LIBERO-Pro, RoboCasa365, and real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements over fixed-action-horizon baselines and existing adaptive methods, including up to 8.7 percentage points in simulation and an increase in average real-world success rate from 53.3\% to 74.4\%.

</details>


### [23] [Agile-WAM: An Agile Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20761)
*Hanchu Zhou,Brendan Lynch,Raman Goyal,Dechen Gao,Begum Kasap,Boqi Zhao,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种敏捷触觉世界动作模型，通过视觉-触觉共享隐空间上的直接流匹配与多时间跨度多模态预测，实现接触丰富机器人控制，在仿真和真实任务中兼顾高成功率与低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉运动策略难以学习支撑有效控制的物理动力学；现有触觉世界动作模型常依赖大规模预训练生成骨干，推理效率低且部署不灵活。此外，视觉与触觉信号时间尺度不同：相邻视觉帧高度相似，而触觉信号在接触时可能突变。

Method: 将视觉和触觉观测编码到共享隐空间，并以其为源进行直接的视觉-触觉到动作流匹配，联合生成动作块以及未来视觉/触觉隐表示；引入多时间跨度多模态预测，对视觉隐表示采用更大时间偏移监督，对触觉隐表示预测下一帧，以捕捉细粒度接触动态。

Result: 在九个仿真和五个真实世界接触丰富操作任务中，成功率优于最强基线，并保持低推理延迟；真实实验中整体成功率相对提升 29.4%，推理延迟为 11.9 ms。

Conclusion: 多模态世界动作模型可以通过敏捷架构实现，适用于精确且高频率的机器人控制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) advance beyond conventional visuomotor policies by jointly predicting future world states and robot actions, enabling the policy to learn physical dynamics that support effective control. However, recent tactile WAMs often rely on large-scale pretrained generative backbones to capture contact-rich physical dynamics, which limit their inference efficiency and flexible deployment. In this paper, we present \ABBR{}, an agile tactile World Action Model for contact-rich robot control. \ABBR{} encodes visual and tactile observations into a shared latent that serves as the source of a direct vision-tactile-to-action flow-matching process, which can jointly generate latent representations of action chunks and future visual/tactile latents. A key observation is that vision and tactile signals evolve at inherently different timescales: adjacent visual frames are often highly similar, whereas tactile signals can change abruptly upon contact. We therefore introduce multi-horizon multimodal prediction in \ABBR{}, which provides supervision for visual latent at a larger temporal offset while predicting the tactile latent in the next frame to capture fine-grained contact dynamics. Across nine simulated and five real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, \ABBR{} demonstrates strong and robust performance, outperforming the strongest baseline in success rate while maintaining low inference latency. In particular, in five real-world experiments, \ABBR{} yields a relative gain of $\textbf{29.4\%}$ in overall success rates while achieving inference latency of $\textbf{11.9 ms}$. These results demonstrate that multimodal WAM can be achieved with an agile architecture suitable for precise and high-frequency robot control. More details are available on our project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [24] [OPTED: On-Policy Fine-Tuning for End-to-End Driving using a Render-Free Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20756)
*Damiano Da Col,Maximilian Igl,Peter Karkus,Kashyap Chitta,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Konrad Schindler,Christos Sakaridis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OPTED，将强化学习与端到端驾驶策略的后训练解耦：先用RL在向量化输入上训练特权教师，再用教师监督预训练学生进行闭环微调，在AlpaSim中显著提升驾驶分数并大幅减少仿真交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅扩大预训练数据收益递减，后训练在自动驾驶等物理AI领域日益重要。端到端驾驶策略通常以开环行为克隆预训练，但闭环部署中的复合误差会使车辆偏离训练分布，增加安全关键事件风险；闭环后训练又需昂贵仿真，尤其对基于传感器的策略。

Method: OPTED先训练一个特权教师：使用RL在HD地图和边界框等向量化输入上学习。随后在闭环后训练中，由该教师为预训练学生提供监督。作者将OPTED应用于TransFuser和VaVAM两个相机模型，在AlpaSim中利用真实驾驶日志的神经重建（3DGS）进行微调。

Result: 驾驶分数分别提升1.6倍和9.5倍。受控实验中，OPTED在闭环性能上可与直接RL后训练相当，但所需仿真交互约少三个数量级，同时更接近人类先验。

Conclusion: OPTED能有效解耦RL与端到端策略后训练，降低闭环后训练的仿真成本，并在提升驾驶性能的同时保持与人类先验接近，为自动驾驶端到端策略的安全闭环部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: As scaling pre-training data alone yields diminishing returns, post-training is becoming increasingly important across physical AI domains such as autonomous driving. End-to-end driving policies are pre-trained in open loop with behavior cloning on human demonstrations. However, compounding errors during closed-loop deployment can take the vehicle outside the training data distribution, increasing the risk of safety-critical incidents. Closed-loop post-training can mitigate this risk but requires costly simulation for sensor-based policies. We propose OPTED (on-policy fine-tuning for end-to-end driving) which decouples reinforcement learning from the post-training of the end-to-end policy: a privileged teacher is trained using RL on vectorized inputs (HD-map and bounding boxes). This teacher then provides supervision to the pre-trained student during closed-loop post-training. We apply OPTED to two camera-based models, TransFuser and VaVAM, and fine-tune them in AlpaSim, using neural reconstructions (3DGS) of real driving logs. Driving scores increase by factors of 1.6$\times$ and 9.5$\times$, respectively. In controlled experiments OPTED matches closed-loop performance with approximately three orders of magnitude fewer simulator interactions than direct RL post-training, while staying closer to the human prior. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [25] [MILER: Semantic Mid-Level Representation for Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning in Unstructured Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20747)
*Thomas Steinecker,Denis Trescher,Alexander Bienemann,Thorsten Luettel,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MILER 提出一种面向非结构化环境的端到端策略框架，通过语义中层表示仿真器与轨迹对齐策略实现感知和控制的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习具有超越人类性能和自学习策略的潜力，但在真实世界自动驾驶中应用仍少，尤其是在非结构化环境下，主要受限于 sim-to-real 迁移困难。

Method: 离线训练阶段，使用自定义语义中层表示（MLR）仿真器，并通过强化学习训练策略网络，其控制输出直接作用于自行车模型；真实部署时，相机和 LiDAR 数据经 BEVFusion 生成与 MLR 仿真器一致的语义鸟瞰图表征，策略网络输出不直接作用于实车，而是采用轨迹对齐策略实现感知与控制的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。

Result: 在包含多种障碍物、发夹弯、最高 33.6 km/h 速度及越野路段的多样化测试赛道上评估；在两辆不同车辆、3.0 km 测试赛道上累计无人干预行驶 17.3 km，且整个软件栈运行于 Jetson AGX Orin。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该方法能有效实现非结构化环境下自动驾驶的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，并具备在真实车辆和嵌入式平台上的可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning constitutes a promising approach owing to its potential for superhuman performance and self-learned policies. However, its application to real-world autonomous driving remains scarce, particularly in unstructured environments, because of the challenges associated with sim-to-real transfer for unstructured environments. In this work, we present MILER, an end-to-end policy framework with zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. During offline training, we employ a custom semantic mid-level representation (MLR) simulator and train the policy network using reinforcement learning, with its control outputs applied directly to a bicycle model. During deployment on the real vehicle, camera and LiDAR data are processed by BEVFusion to generate a semantic bird's-eye-view representation consistent with that of the MLR simulator. The actions generated by the policy network are not applied directly to the real vehicle. Instead, we employ a trajectory-alignment strategy that enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of both perception and control. We extensively evaluate the proposed framework on a diverse test track comprising numerous challenges, including various obstacles, hairpin curves, velocities of up to 33.6 km/h, and off-road sections. In total, we drove 17.3 km with two different vehicles on a 3.0 km test track without human intervention, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, the entire software stack runs on a Jetson AGX Orin.

</details>


### [26] [Underwater Visual Target Tracking with Target-Specific Depth Estimation and Adaptive Model-Fusion Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20731)
*Yuheng Zhou,Haiyang Cheng,Yanqi Feng,Pangkit Fong,Mei Xuan Lee,Marcus Gee,Chongrong Fang,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向AUV的立体视觉伺服框架，通过可靠深度提取与解耦自适应MPC实现水下目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉水下目标跟踪面临深度测量不可靠和目标运动未知的挑战，现有框架难以兼顾鲁棒性与实时性。

Method: 感知端从立体图像中通过目标特定深度提取和卡尔曼滤波获得稳定3D相对状态：利用颜色、视差和时间线索构建目标深度掩膜以选择可靠目标像素，并分别滤波深度测量和检测图像中心。控制端将偏航调节与平移控制解耦，避免计算昂贵的耦合多自由度优化，实现实时平移MPC；平移控制器采用自适应模型融合预测控制，结合恒速与零速目标模型以适应不同目标运动模式，利用历史预测误差更新模型权重，并在执行器、跟随距离和视场约束下计算平移指令。

Result: 通过仿真和真实实验验证了所提框架的有效性，并表明其性能优于现有框架。

Conclusion: 该框架能够稳定提取3D相对状态并实现实时平移MPC，提升水下目标跟踪的鲁棒性与整体性能。

Abstract: Vision-based underwater target tracking is challenged by unreliable depth measurements and unknown target motion. This paper proposes a stereo visual-servoing framework for an autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV). For perception, the framework derives a stable 3D relative state from stereo images through target-specific depth extraction and Kalman filtering. It constructs a target-depth mask from color, disparity, and temporal cues to select reliable target pixels, and then filters the resulting depth measurement and detected image center separately. For control, the framework decouples yaw regulation from translational control, avoiding computationally expensive coupled multi-DOF optimization and enabling real-time translational MPC. The translational controller employs adaptive model-fusion predictive control, combining constant-velocity and zero-velocity target models to accommodate different target-motion patterns. It updates the model weights using historical prediction errors and computes translational commands subject to actuation, following-distance, and field-of-view constraints. Through simulations and real-world experiments, we validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and show it has better performance than existing frameworks.

</details>


### [27] [MoWAM: Explicit Future Motion Prediction for Efficient World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20709)
*Jiayu Wang,Bin Zhu,Yue Yu,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoWAM 用显式未来运动预测替代未来视频生成，在保留 WAM 显式未来建模优势的同时提升效率、鲁棒性与推理时可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 通过引入未来动态提升机器人策略学习，但在推理时显式生成未来视频会带来巨大计算开销；若直接移除未来生成，虽提升效率，却使未来动态仅隐式编码在观测特征中，在分布偏移下鲁棒性受限。因此需要一种既显式表达未来、又高效紧凑的未来表征。

Method: 提出 MoWAM：不再重建完整未来场景或生成未来视频，而是将结构化机器人运动作为未来的紧凑抽象，建模当前场景与交互约束下机器人预期演化。采用 Mixture-of-Transformer 架构，在训练中学习未来视觉动态，并联合预测运动与动作；推理时完全移除视频生成，同时保留显式未来表示。紧凑运动表示还支持高效的推理时扩展：采样多组运动-动作候选，并用运动感知的任务进度验证器进行选择。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 及真实世界操作任务上，MoWAM 取得强分布内性能、更优分布外鲁棒性，以及高于代表性 WAM 基线的真实世界平均成功率。随着探索候选数增加，性能进一步提升，表明显式未来运动可作为推理时扩展的有效且高效基础。

Conclusion: MoWAM 证明显式未来运动预测可替代未来视频生成，在显著降低推理开销的同时保持未来动态建模优势，并为机器人策略学习提供可扩展、鲁棒的推理时选择机制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by incorporating future dynamics, yet explicitly generating future videos at inference introduces substantial computational overhead. Removing future generation improves efficiency, but leaves future dynamics only implicitly encoded in observation features, which can limit robustness under distribution shifts. We propose MoWAM, an efficient WAM that replaces future video generation with explicit future motion prediction. Instead of reconstructing the complete future scene, MoWAM models structured robot motion as a compact abstraction of the future, capturing how the robot is expected to evolve under the current scene and interaction constraints. A Mixture-of-Transformer architecture learns future visual dynamics during training while jointly predicting motion and action, allowing video generation to be removed entirely at inference while retaining an explicit representation of the future. The compact motion representation further enables efficient inference-time scaling by sampling multiple candidates of motion and action pairs and selecting among them with a motion-aware task-progress verifier. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that MoWAM achieves strong in-distribution performance, improved out-of-distribution robustness, and higher average real-world success than representative WAM baselines. In addition, performance improves as more candidates are explored, demonstrating that explicit future motion provides an effective and efficient basis for inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [28] [HOPHY: A Hierarchical Hypergraph Representation for Off-Road Path and Mission Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20694)
*Pranay Meshram,Charuvahan Adhivarahan,Prithvi Poddar,Ehsan Tarkesh Esfahani,Chen Wang,Souma Chowdhury,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOPHY 是一种可复用的层次化越野地形表示，基于超图将地图尺度地形组织为语义区域、关键区域和类型化超边，以支持动态条件下的高效重规划与多机器人任务分配，并已在实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后响应、搜索救援和战术UGV等任务级自主需在地形、智能体类型和目标变化时反复进行路径与任务规划；像素网格搜索在公里级重复查询中代价高，而语义抽象必须在条件变化时保持有效代价与连通性。

Method: 提出 HOPHY（基于超图的层次化越野规划），将地图尺度地形组织为几何连通语义区域（GSNodes）、保持连通性的关键区域（Coarse Regions）以及面向地形、智能体与天气上下文的类型化超边；通过超边交集选择受影响区域及关联边进行状态更新，无需重建整个层次结构。

Result: 在公里级真实越野地图上，HOPHY 达到 100% 规划成功率，与 oracle（像素 A*）相比中位代价偏差小于 0.01%，查询与重规划延迟显著低于像素和抽象基线；在多机器人任务分配（MRTA）中，总计算量较像素 A* 降低 79 倍、较最快抽象基线降低 7.2 倍，任务完工时间与像素 A* 相当。并在 Clearpath Jackal 实机上完成 1.5 km、八任务混合地表户外任务及阻塞触发重规划路线。

Conclusion: HOPHY 表明可复用层次化超图表示能在保持近最优规划质量的同时大幅降低重复规划计算，并支持动态环境下的任务级自主和实机部署。

Abstract: Mission-level autonomy for disaster response, search and rescue, and tactical UGV operations requires repeated path and mission planning as terrain conditions, agent types, and objectives change. Pixel-grid search is costly for repeated kilometer-scale queries, while semantic abstractions must maintain valid costs and connectivity as conditions change. We present HOPHY (Hierarchical Off-Road Planning using Hypergraphs), a reusable hierarchical terrain representation that organizes map-scale terrain into geometrically connected semantic regions (GSNodes), connectivity-preserving critical regions (Coarse Regions), and typed hyperedges for terrain, agent, and weather context. Hyperedge intersections select affected regions and incident edges for state updates without rebuilding the hierarchy. Across real off-road maps spanning kilometer-scale areas, HOPHY achieves 100% planning success and less than 0.01% median cost deviation from the oracle (pixel A*), with substantially lower query and replanning latency than the evaluated pixel and abstraction baselines. Applied to a multi-robot task-allocation (MRTA) problem, these gains reduce total computation by 79x over pixel A* and 7.2x over the fastest abstraction baseline, with mission makespan comparable to pixel A*. Finally, we demonstrate HOPHY on a physical Clearpath Jackal that successfully executes a 1.5-km, eight-task mission across mixed-surface outdoor terrain and a blockage-triggered replanned route.

</details>

*Andrea Capuozzo,Fabio Ruggiero,Vincenzo Lippiello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文为混合空水两栖无人机提出 PX4 固件扩展，实现飞行与水面导航统一任务规划与执行，并通过仿真验证平静与波浪条件下的稳定水面导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水生环境测绘与监测需要能在同一任务中结合飞行与水面航行的混合空水两栖无人机，但现有 PX4 生态缺乏对海上导航模式与差异化航点的统一支持。

Method: 在 PX4 固件中引入手动与自主海上导航模式，集成到标准 PX4 任务管线与 QGroundControl 界面，保留原有飞行功能与安全机制，并支持空中与海上差异化航点的统一规划与执行。

Result: 仿真案例验证了实现的有效性，并表明该框架在平静与波浪条件下均能实现稳定的水面导航。

Conclusion: 该框架可实现混合空水两栖任务的统一规划与执行，为水生环境测绘与监测提供可行的 PX4 扩展方案。

Abstract: Mapping and monitoring aquatic environments can benefit from hybrid aerial-amphibious drones able to combine flight and water-surface navigation within the same mission. This paper presents a PX4 firmware extension for such platforms, introducing manual and autonomous marine navigation modes integrated with the standard PX4 mission pipeline and QGroundControl interface. The proposed framework preserves existing flight functionalities and safety mechanisms while enabling unified planning and execution of hybrid aerial-marine missions with differentiated aerial and marine waypoints. Simulated case studies validate the implementation and demonstrate stable surface navigation under calm and wavy conditions.

</details>


### [30] [Towards Scaling Marine Perception with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20680)
*Haoyu Ma,Onur Bagoren,Anja Sheppard,Elias Fandi,Ashrith Edukulla,Tanner Aslan,Natasha Sieh,Jingyu Song,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作扩展了基于 IsaacSim 的水下感知模拟器 OceanSim，提出合成数据生成管线，用于水下场景的大规模自动标注数据生成，并在真实海胆检测任务上评估 sim-to-real 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中真实标注数据稀缺、采集昂贵且费时，限制了可扩展机器学习；仿真数据有望弥补这一差距，支持水下感知学习任务。

Method: 在 OceanSim 基础上加入 Synthetic Data Generation 管线，支持可配置的场景外观、结构和传感器设置，生成大规模、自动标注、照片级真实感的合成数据集；在真实海胆检测任务中评估不同合成场景变化对 sim-to-real 迁移的影响。

Result: 该管线能够生成大规模自动标注的逼真合成数据；实验表明不同形式的合成场景变化会影响 sim-to-real 性能，并据此总结了主要发现与当前管线局限。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了当前管线的主要限制，并指出未来方向：提升水下渲染保真度、增加场景多样性，以及改进 sim-to-real 泛化评估；代码已开源。

Abstract: Scalable machine learning in challenging underwater environments is strongly limited by the lack of labeled real-world training data. This data is often expensive and laborious to gather, making large-scale real-world data challenging to gather and curate. However, simulated data can help close the gap, enabling many learning-based tasks for underwater perception. In this work, we extend OceanSim, an IsaacSim-based underwater perception simulator, with a Synthetic Data Generation (SDG) pipeline for training models to be used in underwater scenarios. The proposed pipeline enables users to generate large, automatically labeled, photorealistic datasets with configurable scene appearance, structure, and sensor settings. We evaluate the pipeline on a real-world sea urchin detection task and study how different forms of synthetic scene variation affect sim-to-real performance. Based on these experiments, we discuss findings on our results, main limitations of the current pipeline and identify future directions for improving underwater rendering fidelity, scene diversity, and the evaluation of sim-to-real generalization. The open-source code can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [31] [MAGNETAR: Multipath-Guided Spatial Posteriors for Transmitter Pose Inference in the Upper Mid-Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20670)
*Haozhe Lei,Ruibin Chen,Yuhan Jiang,Ali Rasteh,Aditya Dhananjay,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAGNETAR利用单次异步RF多径快照，在给定房间布局与接收机位姿下，推断发射机平面位置与航向的联合后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱房间中，单次测量常与多个发射机位置及多个航向一致；且中高频段天线具方向性，仅用点估计不足，需表达多模态不确定性。

Method: 将AoA与SNR估计作为输入，采用五个神经评分器；最终使用以候选航向为条件的共享2D U-Net，在离散位置-航向网格上联合归一化得分。训练依赖真实到仿真校准的10 GHz仿真数据及少量实测子集。

Result: 在留出仿真中，基于网格的联合后验优于参数化后验；航向条件评分器在机器人实测上迁移最佳；融合联合后验优于融合仅位置边缘后验。

Conclusion: MAGNETAR表明，单快照RF多径下通过航向条件神经评分与网格联合后验，可更稳健地实现位置-航向联合定位，并提升机器人场景中的迁移与多源融合效果。

Abstract: Robots that localize a radio transmitter need more than a point estimate: in cluttered rooms, one measurement is often consistent with several transmitter locations and, because upper-mid-band antennas are directional, several headings. We present MAGNETAR, which infers a joint posterior over planar transmitter position and heading from a single asynchronous radio-frequency (RF) multipath snapshot, represented by angle-of-arrival and signal-to-noise-ratio estimates, given the room layout and receiver pose. Among our five neural scorers, MAGNETAR adopts a shared 2D U-Net conditioned on each candidate heading, jointly normalizing scores over a discretized position-heading grid. Training uses real-to-sim-calibrated 10 GHz simulations and a small measured subset. Grid-based joint posteriors outperform parametric ones on held-out simulations, the heading-conditioned scorer transfers best to robotic measurements, and fusing joint posteriors improves on fusing position-only marginals.

</details>


### [32] [Learning Foresight without Explicit Trajectories for 3D Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20669)
*Zhongbo Zhang,Zaibin Zhang,Yifan Wang,Changbo Yan,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Movement Trend Guidance，在 DP3 基础上从短观察历史学习交互演化的紧凑潜表示，并用稀疏未来夹爪状态监督；推理时仅将潜表示作为面向未来的条件，以极少量参数提升 3D 扩散策略的操纵性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 扩散策略虽能基于当前观测生成几何可行的动作，但成功操纵还需预判交互将如何发展；已有策略多让这种前瞻性从动作学习中隐式涌现，缺乏显式引导。

Method: 从短观察历史中学习交互演化的紧凑潜变量；训练时用稀疏未来夹爪状态监督该表示；推理时保留该潜变量，与当前观测共同作为未来导向条件。潜变量为动作生成提供全局条件，并在 UNet bottleneck 额外通过 gated FiLM 分支注入。相比 DP3 仅增加 3.52% 参数，保持原有 dense-action 与 receding-horizon 形式。

Result: 在 RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-40、DexArt 和真实机器人任务上持续优于 DP3：RoboTwin2.0 50 任务混合训练 62.8% vs 56.1%；LIBERO-40 71.93% vs 37.08%；五类真实机器人任务 72.0% vs 49.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，扩散策略无需被告知精确移动目标，仅需知道交互的发展趋势即可显著受益；该方法以简单有效的方式为 3D 扩散策略提供未来导向先验，同时保持原策略范式。

Abstract: 3D diffusion policies are strong at generating geometrically grounded actions from current observations, but successful manipulation requires not only knowing what motion is feasible now, but also anticipating where the interaction is heading. Existing policies largely leave such foresight to emerge implicitly from action learning. We introduce Movement Trend Guidance, a simple but effective way to provide this foresight without introducing an explicit plan. From a short observation history, the policy learns a compact latent representation of interaction evolution. During training, sparse future gripper states supervise this representation; at inference, only the latent is retained as future-oriented conditioning alongside the current observation. The latent provides global conditioning for action generation, while an additional gated FiLM branch is used only at the UNet bottleneck. Despite adding only 3.52% more parameters to DP3, our method preserves the original dense-action and receding-horizon formulation and consistently improves upon DP3 across RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-40, and DexArt. It reaches 62.8% vs. 56.1% in 50-task RoboTwin2.0 mixed training, 71.93% vs. 37.08% on LIBERO-40, and 72.0% vs. 49.0% on five real-robot tasks. These results show that a diffusion policy can benefit substantially from knowing where an interaction is heading, without being told exactly where to move.

</details>


### [33] [HIL-UMI: Bringing Human-in-the-Loop Post-Training of Vision-Language-Action Models to Universal Manipulation Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20659)
*Zimu Han,Yiming Zeng,Jiyao Zhang,Zihao Zhao,Yuanfei Wang,Yixiang Jin,Shiqi Li,Shuangben Chen,Wei Huang,Ruodai Li,Hui Shen,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HIL-UMI 提出一种无需真实机器人执行、基于手持 UMI 演示的人类在环 VLA 后训练框架，通过策略引导采集与优势估计精炼，在多个真实操作任务上稳定超越 SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽提供强操作先验，但适配具体部署仍困难。任务示范上的监督微调（SFT）存在两个问题：静态数据对分布外状态覆盖有限；标准模仿目标无法区分推进性行为与低价值数据。交互式后训练可缓解这些问题，但通常需要物理机器人反复执行策略并进行人工干预。

Method: HIL-UMI 是一种策略引导的通用操作接口（UMI）框架，用于无机器人的人类在环 VLA 后训练。在手持 UMI 演示过程中，当前策略在同一观测流上被查询但不执行其预测。Energy Score 比较人类动作轨迹与策略推理，当差异表明处于分布外区域时触发数据采集。另一反馈回路利用低在线优势预测识别关键片段，以精炼基于进度的优势估计器。更新后的估计器以基础示范和新策略数据的平衡混合，引导优势条件行为克隆。该设计保留人类在环学习的迭代性与策略感知特性，同时将数据采集与机器人部署解耦。

Result: 在涵盖长时程与精细操作的四项真实任务上，HIL-UMI 相比 SFT 取得一致提升，并受益于定向采集与优势精炼。此外，在 Clean Up Table 任务上，HIL-UMI 优于 HG-DAgger，且每帧采集时间更低。

Conclusion: HIL-UMI 为跨操作者与地点的 VLA 后训练提供了一条可扩展路径，表明无需物理机器人执行即可实现策略感知、人类在环的数据采集与策略改进。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models provide powerful priors for robot manipulation, yet adapting them to a specific deployment remains challenging. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on task-specific demonstrations provides a step toward deployment, but faces two persistent limitations: static data provide limited coverage of out-of-distribution states, and standard imitation objectives do not distinguish progressing behavior from less useful data. Interactive post-training can address these limitations, but typically requires repeated policy execution and human intervention on a physical robot. We introduce HIL-UMI, a policy-guided Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) framework for robot-free human-in-the-loop VLA post-training. During handheld UMI demonstrations, HIL-UMI queries the current policy on the same observation stream without executing its predictions. The Energy Score compares the human action trajectory with policy inference and triggers collection when their discrepancy indicates an out-of-distribution region. In a separate feedback loop, low online advantage predictions identify essential segments for refining a progress-based advantage estimator. The updated estimator then guides advantage-conditioned behavioral cloning using a balanced mixture of base demonstrations and new policy data. This design preserves the iterative and policy-aware nature of human-in-the-loop learning while decoupling data collection from robot deployment. Experiments on four real-world tasks spanning long-horizon and precise manipulation show that HIL-UMI achieves consistent improvement over SFT and benefits from both targeted collection and advantage refinement. Moreover, HIL-UMI outperforms HG-DAgger on Clean Up Table with lower per-frame collection time, suggesting a scalable path for VLA post-training across operators and locations.

</details>


### [34] [DexTouch-WM: Learning Action-Conditioned Tactile World Models from Human Touch for Dexterous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20649)
*Yan Qin,Yue Chen,Wenwei Lin,Shujia Liu,Chuqiao Lyu,Kailun Su,Chenze Yu,Ping Luo,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DexTouch-WM，一种动作条件世界模型，利用可扩展的人类触觉数据联合预测未来 RGB 观测与双手触觉动力学，以缓解真实机器人密集触觉数据稀缺和具身传感器依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的灵巧操作预测模型依赖密集触觉交互，但真实机器人上此类数据难以规模化，且受限于特定具身传感器；作者希望利用人类触觉交互作为可扩展数据轴，学习可迁移到机器人的接触动力学。

Method: 在人类手与灵巧机器人手上部署共享传感布局的柔性压阻阵列，并将人类动作重定向到机器人动作空间，使人类交互能监督同一动力学模型。DexTouch-WM 将预训练视频专家与轻量触觉专家耦合，采用解剖学感知触觉 token 和对齐动作条件，联合预测未来 RGB 观测与双侧触觉动力学。

Result: 在人类到机器人缩放实验中，固定 5 小时真实机器人监督，将人类交互从 0 小时增至 100 小时；尽管人类与机器人任务集不相交，模型在留出机器人域上的视觉、几何和接触预测均显著提升。世界模型还被用作策略评估代理环境，并生成合成轨迹用于真实机器人策略学习。

Conclusion: 可扩展的人类交互为学习灵巧机器人世界模型提供了互补数据轴，表明在触觉观测与动作空间兼容时，人类与机器人操作可共享可迁移接触动力学。

Abstract: Learning predictive models of contact-rich dexterous manipulation requires dense tactile interaction, but such data are costly to scale on real robots and remain tied to embodiment-specific sensors. We introduce DexTouch-WM, an action-conditioned world model that learns from scalable human touch to jointly predict future RGB observations and bilateral tactile dynamics. Our insight is that human and robot manipulation share transferable contact dynamics when their tactile observations and action spaces are made compatible. We deploy flexible piezoresistive arrays with a shared sensing layout on both human and dexterous robot hands, and retarget human motion into the robot action space so that human interaction can supervise the same dynamics model used for real-robot prediction. DexTouch-WM couples a pretrained video expert with a lightweight tactile expert using anatomy-aware tactile tokens and aligned action conditioning. In human-to-robot scaling experiments, we keep five hours of real-robot supervision fixed while increasing human interaction from 0 to 100 hours, and observe substantial improvements in held-out robot-domain visual, geometric, and contact prediction despite disjoint human and robot task sets. Beyond prediction, we evaluate the world models as surrogate environments for policy evaluation and as generators of synthetic trajectories for real-robot policy learning, showing that scalable human interaction provides a complementary data axis for learning dexterous robot world models.

</details>


### [35] [SkipVLA: Skipping VLA Steps with Classical Planning for Fast Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20648)
*Kaivalya Agrawal,Md Ashiqur Rahman,Raymond A. Yeh,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkipVLA 是一种混合机器人策略，将预训练 VLA 与经典运动规划器结合：自由空间运动由规划器处理，仅在抓取、放置等接触丰富技能时调用 VLA，从而在保持成功率的同时显著降低延迟与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽可直接由图像和语言指令映射到动作，但推理速度慢，长时程任务需频繁查询策略。现有降延迟方法仍对整项任务运行学习策略；而经典运动规划器虽能快速生成无碰撞运动，却需要显式目标且缺乏任务语义理解。

Method: SkipVLA 使用经典运动规划器完成自由空间运动，仅对接触丰富技能查询预训练 VLA。它复用 VLA 冻结的视觉-语言骨干网络，为每个规划运动预测目标位姿，并利用大型 VLA 已有知识学习该预测器，无需额外演示数据。

Result: 在仿真中基于 3 个 VLA 在 13 个 LIBERO 任务上评估，并在物理 6-DoF YAM 机械臂上完成 3 个抓放任务。结果显示任务完成速度最高提升 2.5 倍，能耗显著降低，同时任务成功率相同。

Conclusion: SkipVLA 通过结合 VLA 的语义理解与经典运动规划器的快速无碰撞运动能力，有效减少测试时延迟和能耗，并保持任务成功率，为长时程机器人操作提供了高效混合策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a class of generalist robot policies that map camera images and language instructions directly to robot actions. While promising, these models remain slow at test time, particularly for long-horizon tasks that require many queries to the policy. Recent efforts reduce VLA latency by distilling smaller models, overlapping asynchronous action chunks, or pairing the VLA with a fast low-level policy, but still run a learned policy for the entire task. In contrast to VLA, classical motion planners quickly find collision-free motions, but require an explicit goal and have no semantic understanding of the task. In this work, we present SkipVLA, a hybrid policy that combines a pretrained VLA with a classical motion planner, using the planner for free-space motion and querying the VLA only for contact-rich skills such as grasping and placing. SkipVLA reuses the frozen vision-language backbone of the VLA to predict a target pose for each planned motion, and learns this predictor without additional demonstrations introduced into the system by using what was already learnt by the large VLA. We evaluate SkipVLA with three VLAs on 13 LIBERO tasks in simulation and three pick-and-place tasks on a physical 6-DoF YAM arm, demonstrating up to 2.5x faster task completion and significantly lower energy consumption while achieving the same task success rate.

</details>


### [36] [TraceFlow: Guiding Frozen Flow-Matching Robot Policies with Success and Failure Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20646)
*Jiaxuan Zhang,Ruizhe Liu,Yu Zhang,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO
